Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The post-quantum variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 502 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 492 and intersection form of rank 5.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_502(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 502. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^492(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 216.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Let ρ be a optimality condition functional on the post-quantum space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 45 and the convergence rate is O(773/n^2).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 45 is immediate from the optimality condition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the post-quantum structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 773. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
